P177b: 0 provider calls. Figure binding is one rule, compile.bind(); the acceptance is the user's expected_outcome of the 281 rows
(kb/reports/P177a_panels.csv, copied from the user's P177a_panels_expected.csv): 247 match, 34 do not, 27 of them data panels. The unit is
not done; no apply.

0. ontology.yaml layouts: (curve_per_unit, point_per_unit, panel_one_unit; row_per_unit, not_units) with item, conditions_from,
   results_from and the P143 text; export.py prints them into the pass-0 instruction, form_pass0.json (enum + 'other' + description) and
   CODEBOOK.md (the P143 constants gone; grow.py's symbol-table rule reads them too). A pass-0 layout 'other' -> an unmapped row of field
   layout -> a candidate of kind layout; apply refuses to accept one (a layout kind goes into ontology.yaml by hand). export --check 15/15.
1. compile.bind() replaces curve_units, panel_items, legend_conditions (now parse_legend, which classes each part by what it resolves
   to: value, species, name, qualifier, quantity, model, data role, method, never, other), method_panels, the role fallbacks and the unit
   merge. Items per layout (schematic none; image panel one image item with its crop; a data panel with nothing read one panel item; a
   map with cells its cells; no layout unbound). Unit: pass 0's data ref; a panel or image item the layout's unit, else the one unit the
   panel, else the figure names; a model name a model run under the model's simulation; else the one pass-0 unit whose conditions (the
   pass-1 settings about it and its parent) contain the item's resolved conditions; else a derived unit (role from an explicit simulated /
   fit mark, else the unit list, else null flagged). Results: compile.figures reads bind's items (a curve its y or the legend's quantity
   with its qualifiers and method; a point its value; a panel its curves; an image a statement-only observation under the measurement).
   Every item bound or unbound with a reason (binding_check; pass0_paper.consistency and the gate count the unbound: reference 0 -> 11,
   the new count; the pass-1 references kept, see 3).
   Beyond the directive, my choices: (a) pass 0's data ref binds first (the 'as now' rows); (b) the vector match runs only over the units
   the figure names when it names any (else ela03 fig5b's 1 s point binds silently to the Al2O3 specimen) and an ancestor yields to its
   descendant; (c) one derived unit per (role, model, vector) within a panel, not across the paper: across panels hei24's legends repeat
   for runs that differ in the caption, and the paper-wide merge dropped hei24 diff_entity 83 -> 56 and diff_setting 73 -> 42 (no column
   may fall); an id merged in a panel stays its unit's alias (pass 1's records name it); (d) a species name with no axis is a species
   qualifier (zou23 fig4 'H2O'); (e) a repeated panel id (gao15 fig1 x3, zou23 fig4 x2) keeps its panels apart (pel~2).
2. Deleted: the P162 per-point rule, the P161/P176 role fallbacks, the method-legend special case, dedupe_units / merge_units.
3. Acceptance mismatches (every row with its cause in table 3): ambiguous naming (ela03 fig3, gor03 fig2, art19 fig2A, gre23 fig5, mat23
   fig3a, fig3b, fig4a: the figure names several units); data (ela03 fig5b-11b and zou23 fig1a/c/d: an origin point x = 0 and conditions
   pass 1 never recorded; zou23 fig5b: 350 °C is the run and four specimens); vocabulary (zou23 fig2: 'SE' no method alias; yim20 fig7:
   'X5' names nothing; yim22 fig6a: 'D_A' is the never word 'dA; dB'; yim22 fig6b-d: 'cext' is a yim22 symbol of initial_sticking_coefficient);
   rule (mat23 fig4b: '@' joins name and value); premise (wer21 fig4-8: the figure stage holds no curve; fig4 16 peaks); artifacts (ela03
   'fig41 s' maps: image items, not 'no layout'; zou23's second fig4 panel holds no data).
   Scores of the twelve (R5 -> P177b): no column fell; T6 2074 -> 2115, gold rows 5357; check.py 2212 -> 2512 (points_ref in a facts file
   +154: every point and image fact carries points_ref; same statement +55; growth_per_cycle without over= +47; value without quantity +36;
   zou23 +186). Audits 1-5 PASS. Cards: the thirteen pages, 0 errors (zou23 72 compiled cards, ela03 50). Gate pass 1: zou23's form
   failures share rose to 0.145 (its new point settings of pulse_time / purge_time lack the step qualifier form.json requires, dropped at
   step a); the reference kept at 0.091, so a run would stop on such a paper. kb/reports/binding_regression.csv: 798 items; grow.py apply
   re-checks it (kb/extract/binding.py --check: an item it binds must keep its unit) and writes it anew.

+def data_role_word(part):
+    """P177b: the data role a legend part names when its words are data-role words only ('Simulation', 'Fitting result', 'Linear Fits',
+    'measured'): simulated | fitted | measured; else ''."""
+    ws = re.findall(r"[A-Za-z]+", (part or "").lower())
+    roles = [DATA_ROLE_WORDS[w] for w in ws if w in DATA_ROLE_WORDS]
+    return roles[0] if roles and all(w in DATA_ROLE_WORDS or w in DATA_ROLE_FILLER for w in ws) else ""
+
+
+def parse_legend(pid, label, axis):
+    """P163 rule 1, P175, P177b (user): a legend label's parts (legend_parts at top-level commas; an axis of two or more parts (' and ',
+    ',') pairs with them by order, the legend split at ' - ' / ' – ' when its commas do not give that count), each resolved on the one path
+    and classed by the kind of what it resolved to:
+      value     '<symbol> = <number> [unit]', '<name>: <number> [unit]', '<number> [unit]', '<symbol> = <a> to <b> [unit]' (value_min /
+                value_max): the quantity of its own symbol or name, else of its axis part (vocab_hits, as step a0); unresolved: other;
+      species   a species name on a material / reactant axis; name: a structure or categorical value;
+      qualifier a value of the qualifier the axis names (element, species, step): no unit boundary, on the item's result;
+      quantity  the part names a quantity entry, no number: the item's y quantity (no unit boundary);
+      model     the part names a model entry (name, alias, a symbol of this paper): the item is a model run of that model;

+CONDITION_KINDS = ("value", "name", "species", "other", "never")
+
+
+def cond_key(c):
+    """P177b: a condition as a comparable key: (quantity, canonical value or value range or text); an other or never condition by its
+    label and printed value. None for a part that is no condition."""
+    if c["kind"] not in CONDITION_KINDS:
+        return None
+    if c["kind"] in ("other", "never") or c.get("quantity") in ("", "other"):
+        return ("other", C.X.norm_label(c["key"]), str(c.get("value", "")), str(c.get("value_min", "")), str(c.get("value_max", "")), c.get("unit") or "")
+    if c["kind"] in ("name", "species"):
+        return (c["quantity"], "text", str(c.get("value_text", "")).casefold())
+    if c.get("value") is None:
+        return (c["quantity"], "range", c.get("value_min"), c.get("value_max"), c.get("unit") or "")
+    k = canon(dict(value=num_text(c["value"]), unit=dimless(unit_symbol(c.get("unit") or "") or (c.get("unit") or "")), quantity=c["quantity"]))
+    return (c["quantity"], "value", k)
+
+
+def same_value(a, b):
+    """P177b: two condition keys equal: numbers within 0.1 % (canonical units), else equal."""
+    if a[:2] != b[:2]:
+        return False
+    if a[1] == "value" and isinstance(a[2], tuple) and isinstance(b[2], tuple) and len(a[2]) == 2 and len(b[2]) == 2 and a[2][0] == b[2][0]:
+        x, y = a[2][1], b[2][1]
+        return isinstance(x, float) and isinstance(y, float) and abs(x - y) <= 1e-3 * max(abs(x), abs(y), 1e-300)
+    return a == b
+

+            c = dict(kind="value", key="", quantity=q, value=rec["value"], unit=rec.get("unit") or "")

+        else:
+            continue
+        try:
+            own[rec["about"]].append(cond_key(c))
+        except (TypeError, ValueError):
+            continue

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""P177b (user): the binding regression. kb/reports/binding_regression.csv holds compile.bind()'s outcome for every item of every
+compiled paper (compile.binding_rows: paper, panel, item, kind, unit, origin, role, how, reason). Every later apply re-checks it
+(grow.py apply_here): an item the file binds to a unit must still be bound to that unit; an item it lists unbound may since be bound
+(that is no failure). No model call.
+
+    python3 psed_v2/kb/extract/binding.py --check     # exit 1 and the differences when an item lost its unit
+    python3 psed_v2/kb/extract/binding.py --write     # the file anew from the compiled papers
+"""
+import csv
+import sys
+
+import common as C
+import compile as K
+
+FILE = C.V2 / "kb" / "reports" / "binding_regression.csv"
+FIELDS = ["paper", "panel", "item", "kind", "unit", "origin", "role", "how", "reason"]
+
+
+def current():
+    """The outcome rows of every paper with records and compiled facts."""
+    out = []
+    for pid in C.E.PAPERS:
+        if (C.RECORDS / f"{pid}.jsonl").exists() and (K.OUT / f"{pid}.facts.csv").exists():
+            run = K.Run(pid)
+            run.read()
+            out += K.binding_rows(pid, run.binding)
+    return out
+
+
+def check(rows):
+    """The items the file binds that are now unbound or bound to another unit."""
+    if not FILE.exists():
+        return []
+    now = {(r["paper"], r["item"]): r for r in rows}
+    bad = []
+    for r in csv.DictReader(FILE.open(encoding="utf-8")):
+        if not r["unit"]:
+            continue
+        n = now.get((r["paper"], r["item"]))
+        if n is None or n["unit"] != r["unit"]:
+            bad.append(f"{r['paper']} {r['item']}: was {r['unit']} ({r['how']}), now " + (f"{n['unit'] or 'unbound: ' + n['reason']}" if n else "no such item"))
+    return bad
+
+
+def write(rows):
+    with FILE.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=FIELDS, lineterminator="\n")
+        w.writeheader()
+        w.writerows(rows)
+
+
+def main(argv):
+    rows = current()
+    if "--check" in argv:
+        bad = check(rows)
+        for b in bad:
+            print(b)
+        print(f"binding regression: {len(rows)} items, {len(bad)} lost their unit")
+        return 1 if bad else 0
+    if "--write" in argv:
+        write(rows)
+        print(f"binding regression: {len(rows)} items written to {FILE}")
+        return 0
+    print(__doc__)
+    return 2
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))
